- 所有 eval 必须 `--no-quantize`（D058：SFT baseline 推理量化 confound，之前量化 eval 数据不可比）
- CED 不再作为独立贡献（D056：CED vs flat-NLI 统计不可区分，p=0.08/0.38/0.12）。论文中 CED 降级为 "one possible scoring function"，不可声称 CED > flat-NLI
- GRPO decoupling 仅限 "QLoRA-specific"（D054），禁止说 "online RL fails for structured IE"

### 3. 📊 当前状态
4 块 GPU 全部在跑，无空闲：

| GPU | exp_id | 任务 | 状态 |
|-----|--------|------|------|
| cuda:0 | eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43 | flat-NLI RSFT s43 dev eval | QUEUED（已提交，PID 252179） |
| cuda:1 | plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval | bf16 GRPO step 30/50/70 eval | RUNNING（第3次提交，前2次因 device_map=auto 占 GPU0 被 OOM kill，已修复为 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1） |
| cuda:2 | eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep | DPO+CED 1ep checkpoint dev eval | RUNNING |
| cuda:3 | plan_008_rsft_topk3 | RSFT top_k=3 ablation 训练（8037 samples, 3ep） | RUNNING |

PREPARING 中有 2 个残留条目（mvp2_eval_rsft_s44、plan_011_eval_g8_bf16_ckpt100），均标注 STALE/killed，不需执行。

**等待回调**：3 个 cron 在监控上述 RUNNING 实验，完成后会触发 `[CRON]` 回调。无等待中的 Worker 回调或 Director 决策。

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43 完成** → 对比 CED RSFT (rel_f1=0.4449/0.4655) vs flat-NLI RSFT。这是关键分叉点：CED≈flat-NLI 已由 D056 确认，此 eval 提供 s43 数据点。完成后解锁 plan_002 (test set eval) 和 plan_003 (EDCR pipeline)
- **eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep 完成** → 对比 SFT baseline (rel_f1=0.4057)。Reviewer 已 WARNING 过拟合信号（3ep rewards_acc=1.0, margins=9x），看 1ep 是否改善。结果入 paper C2 (offline preference baseline)
- **plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval 完成** → step 30/50/70 的 rel_f1/evi_f1 曲线，用于 forgetting dynamics figure (plan_009)。C2 证据链的补充数据点
- **plan_008_rsft_topk3 训练完成** → 需要 spawn worker 做 dev eval（类似其他 eval 命令），对比 top_k=1 RSFT (rel_f1=0.4449)，验证 top_k 对 RSFT 质量的影响
- **所有 4 个 eval 收齐** → 派 Paper Agent 做 retitle + reframe + related work 扩展（ROADMAP 中标记的任务）
- **plan_009 forgetting dynamics figure**：ROADMAP 标记 P0，需检查是否已有 worker 完成。数据依赖 plan_011 step eval 结果 + 之前 QLoRA GRPO 的 wandb metrics

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心结论链**：
- D054: GRPO evidence-extraction decoupling 是 QLoRA 量化噪声导致，非 RL 通病。bf16 GRPO (rel_f1=0.4549) 正常，QLoRA GRPO (rel_f1=0.0138) catastrophic
- D056: CED vs flat-NLI 统计不可区分 → C3 降级，论文核心贡献收窄为 "task definition + decoupling finding + EDCR metric"
- D058: SFT baseline 之前的 eval 用了量化推理，有 confound。所有新 eval 必须 `--no-quantize`
- D059: Claim 2 re-scope，decoupling 作为负面发现保留

**论文定位**：EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25)。剩余约 2 个月。当前处于 "核心 eval 收齐" 阶段，收齐后进入写作。

**易错点**：
- plan_011 已经失败 2 次（device_map=auto 导致模型加载到 GPU0 和别的实验撞 OOM），第 3 次已修复为显式 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1。如果再失败，检查 eval 脚本是否正确使用了环境变量
- report.md 中大量告警条目是 MVP2 阶段的历史残留（mvp2_* 系列），均已终结，不需处理
- ROADMAP 中 "plan_014 SFT-no-evidence eval (cuda:1 运行中)" 可能已完成或被 plan_011 取代了 cuda:1，需确认 plan_014 的 registry 状态
- eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43 状态为 QUEUED 但已有 PID，可能实际已在运行，cron 未覆盖——接手后优先 `poll_cron` 确认

```json
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01MZpoQSDSnkPzTthkWNyCQi`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

```text
Now I understand the resource-saturation requirements. Let me assess:

**A. GPU 分配率**: 4 GPUs allocated, 4 running. All saturated. ✅
- cuda:0: eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43 (RUNNING)
- cuda:1: plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval (RUNNING)


- cuda:2: eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep (RUNNING)
- cuda:3: plan_008_rsft_topk3 (RUNNING)

**B. GPU 效率**: All 4 experiments are active with crons, though I can't verify efficiency metrics without health checks.

**C. 非 GPU 任务并行**: Worker pool is at 0, which violates the < 2 rule—I need to spawn workers for CPU tasks. Looking at what I can process now, eval_plan_014_sft_no_evidence has significant results that need attention.

The eval results are striking: SFT without evidence achieves rel_f1 = 0.4893, outperforming SFT with evidence at 0.4106, while various RSFT configurations range from 0.4449 to 0.4655, and bf16 GRPO sits at 0.4549. I should report these findings to the Director before diving into deeper analysis, and I can update the ROADMAP once plan_009_forgetting_figure analysis is complete, though paper-related work still needs to wait until all evaluations finish. This is striking: the best relation extraction performance is from the model that doesn't generate evidence at all (0.4893). All evidence-generating models are lower. This could change the paper narrative significantly.
```

```json
{
  "task_type": "research:evidence-cost-literature",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：文献调研 — Evidence Generation 对 IE/RE 性能的影响\n\n### 背景\n我们的实验发现：在 DocRE（文档级关系抽取）任务中，训练模型同时输出 relation triples + evidence sentences（即 evidence-grounded RE）比只输出 relation triples 的 rel_f1 低约 8pp（0.4106 vs 0.4893）。这说明 evidence generation 对 relation extraction 有显著的性能代价。\n\n同时，CED reranker（推理时从 N=8 SFT 候选中用 NLI 模型选最佳）几乎消除了这个 gap（rel_f1=0.4864）且保留了 evidence 输出（evi_f1=0.8188）。\n\n### 调研方向\n搜索以下方向的相关论文（2022-2026）：\n1. **Evidence/rationale generation 对主任务的性能影响**: 特别是在 IE/RE/NER 任务中，要求模型同时生成解释/证据是否降低主任务性能\n2. **Multi-task learning trade-off in structured prediction**: 生成式模型同时输出结构化结果+辅助信息时的性能折衷\n3. **Faithfulness vs accuracy trade-off**: 在 NLP 中追求可解释性/可追溯性是否会牺牲准确性\n4. **Reranking/verification 作为 bridge**: 用后处理（如 NLI-based reranking）在不牺牲主任务性能的前提下获得高质量 evidence\n\n### 输出要求\n1. 找到 5-10 篇最相关的论文\n2. 对每篇论文：标题、作者、年份、venue、核心发现（1-2 句）\n3. 与我们工作的关联（如何在 Related Work 中引用）\n4. 将结果更新到项目的 literature_map.yaml 中（用 save_project_file）\n\n### 工具\n- 用 WebSearch 搜索论文\n- 用 read_project_file 读取现有 literature_map.yaml\n- 用 save_project_file 更新 literature_map.yaml（file=\"literature_map.yaml\"）\n",
  "tool_groups": [
    "knowledge"
  ],
  "tools": [
    "WebSearch"
  ]
}
```
